FinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming
Authors: Chaeyun Kim, Daeyoung Park, Junghwan Kim, Jinyoung Jeong, Eunji Song, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
First: 2026-06-18T07:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-22T05:37:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE ICDM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinRED：一个专家指导的金融LLM红队基准生成与评估框架
现有的安全基准针对一般对抗场景，但忽视了金融特定风险。金融LLM面临合规性违规、欺诈促进和系统性信任侵蚀等问题，需要针对性评估。我们引入了FinRED，这是一个由金融专家开发的专家指导的红队框架，用于金融LLM安全评估。FinRED使用一种新颖的两级分类法，将全球标准（如FATF和EU DORA）映射到从监管规避到复杂欺诈的威胁，结合一个可扩展的管道，通过专家定义的模式将真实金融文档转换为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子）。严格的专家验证确认了种子的合理性和真实性，以便进行有意义的LLM安全评估。我们还提供了一个经过专家验证的金融特定评分标准，超越了免责声明检查，更加贴近人类专家，而不是静态的一刀切评分标准，并将关键的假阴性从28减少到12。FinRED与国际公认的风险管理和信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）保持一致，已在韩国金融安全研究所（FSI）的监管沙箱中部署，用于真实金融服务中的生成AI安全评估。为了减轻双重用途风险，数据集、生成管道、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究人员进行了限制，网址为https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper和https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the inadequacy of existing safety benchmarks for financial LLMs, which often overlook finance-specific risks such as regulatory compliance violations and fraud. Previous methods have failed to provide targeted evaluations, leading to critical false negatives in safety assessments. The proposed FinRED framework, developed with input from financial experts, introduces a two-level taxonomy that aligns global standards with specific threats, and utilizes a scalable pipeline to create context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts from real financial documents. The contribution of this research lies in its expert-validated rubric that significantly reduces false negatives and enhances the evaluation of LLM safety in financial contexts. The methodology involves rigorous expert validation and deployment in a regulatory sandbox, achieving improved performance metrics that support the framework's goals of ensuring safety in generative AI applications within financial services.
本研究解决了现有金融大型语言模型（LLM）安全基准不足的问题，现有基准往往忽视了诸如合规违规和欺诈等金融特定风险。以往的方法主要集中在一般对抗场景，未能针对金融领域的独特挑战提供评估。提出的FinRED框架通过采用两级分类法，将全球标准映射到特定威胁，结合专家见解创建上下文丰富的红队行为提示，从而与现有方法区分开来。该方法的动机充分，因为它与国际风险管理标准一致，并经过专家验证以确保评估种子的真实性。本文的贡献在于其专家指导的框架，将安全评估中的关键假阴性从28减少到12，并已在韩国金融安全研究所部署，用于现实世界的生成式人工智能安全评估，证明了其在增强金融LLM安全评估方面的有效性。
BreakFun: Jailbreaking LLMs via Object Instantiation under Simulated Code Execution
Authors: Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei, Mehmet S. Aktas
First: 2025-10-19T11:27:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-18T18:53:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AACL-IJCNLP 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used because they process structures, syntax and code well, but this same ability also makes them paradoxically vulnerable. We introduce BreakFun, a jailbreak method that frames a harmful request as code-execution simulation. The prompt gives the model a benign Python class definition, the "Trojan Schema", and asks what that code would print if it ran. To answer, the model must create an object from the class and invent a value for each field, and the adversarial field names steer those values toward the attacker's goal. The harmful content therefore appears as a side-effect of the simulated instantiation, not as a direct answer. A three-part prompt carries the attack: an innocent frame, the Trojan Schema, and a Chain-of-Thought distraction. On JailbreakBench, BreakFun reaches an average attack success rate of 89% across 13 open-weight and commercial models (open-weight ~98%, API systems ~78%), and 100% on several. An ablation identifies the Trojan Schema as the strongest contributing component. As a counter-ablation probe we introduce Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD), in which a second LLM writes out all readable text in the prompt before it judges safety. APD improves detection across three model families, and a probe-specific ablation shows that the transcription step gives most of the gain. Our findings show that LLM safety must account for content that a simulation task produces as a side-effect, not only for content that a user requests directly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BreakFun：通过模拟代码执行下的对象实例化破解大型语言模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）因其良好的结构、语法和代码处理能力而被广泛使用，但这种能力也使其在某种程度上变得脆弱。我们介绍了BreakFun，这是一种将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟的越狱方法。提示给模型一个无害的Python类定义，即“特洛伊模式”，并询问如果运行该代码会打印什么。为了回答，模型必须从类中创建一个对象并为每个字段发明一个值，而对抗性字段名称则将这些值引导至攻击者的目标。因此，有害内容作为模拟实例化的副作用出现，而不是直接答案。攻击通过三部分提示进行：一个无辜的框架、特洛伊模式和一个思维链干扰。在JailbreakBench上，BreakFun在13个开放权重和商业模型中达到了89%的平均攻击成功率（开放权重约98%，API系统约78%），并在多个模型上达到了100%。消融实验确定特洛伊模式是最强的贡献成分。作为对抗消融探针，我们引入了对抗性提示解构（APD），其中第二个LLM在判断安全性之前写出提示中的所有可读文本。APD提高了三个模型家族的检测能力，而特定探针的消融显示转录步骤带来了大部分增益。我们的研究结果表明，LLM安全性必须考虑模拟任务作为副作用产生的内容，而不仅仅是用户直接请求的内容。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to harmful requests due to their proficiency in processing code and structured data. Previous methods for mitigating these vulnerabilities have not effectively prevented adversarial exploitation, prompting the development of BreakFun, which employs a novel approach by framing harmful requests as code-execution simulations. This method allows harmful content to emerge as a side-effect of object instantiation rather than a direct response, thus circumventing traditional safety measures. The paper contributes a new jailbreak technique that achieves an average attack success rate of 89% across various models on the JailbreakBench, with specific components like the Trojan Schema identified as key contributors to its effectiveness. Additionally, the introduction of Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD) enhances detection capabilities, demonstrating that LLM safety must consider indirect outputs from simulation tasks.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）因其有效处理代码和结构的能力而面临的脆弱性。以往的缓解方法往往不足，因为它们未能充分考虑LLMs被操控的微妙方式。提出的BreakFun方法创新性地将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟，利用一个称为“特洛伊模式”的无害Python类定义来引导模型的输出朝向攻击者的目标，而不是直接提示。这种方法具有良好的动机，因为它利用了LLMs的固有能力，同时规避了传统的安全措施。该方法论涉及一个三部分的提示，包括无害框架、特洛伊模式和思维链干扰。BreakFun在JailbreakBench上在各种模型中实现了89%的平均攻击成功率，其中一些模型达到100%，证明了其在利用LLMs脆弱性方面的有效性，同时强调了需要考虑在模拟任务中生成的副作用内容的安全措施。
Local Sparsity Enables Unsupervised LLM Safety Detection
Authors: Xin Chen, Gil Kur, Alexander Shevchenko, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployment-time safety methods for large language models (LLMs) are predominantly supervised and assume access to unsafe training data. Nevertheless, new attacks and harm categories regularly arise, not captured by models trained in such a supervised fashion. An alternative approach is to view this problem through the lens of anomaly detection, namely, to rely solely on modeling safe data and flagging out-of-distribution inputs. However, LLM activations lie in a high-dimensional space, raising concerns about whether anomaly detection is statistically feasible. We show that, under the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), there may indeed be hope. In the LRH concept space, which is typically recovered via a sparse autoencoder (SAE), nearby points share a small common active support. Using this local sparsity insight, we propose a framework for locally masked SAE-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. We validate it on various architectures and datasets, including both capability-testing datasets and safety-specific datasets. Finally, when we allow algorithms to use 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, demonstrating their ability to capture meaningful safety information while using only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部稀疏性使无监督LLM安全检测成为可能
大型语言模型（LLMs）的部署时安全方法主要是监督性的，并假设可以访问不安全的训练数据。然而，新攻击和伤害类别不断出现，这些在以这种监督方式训练的模型中并未被捕获。另一种方法是通过异常检测的视角来看待这个问题，即仅依赖于建模安全数据并标记分布外输入。然而，LLM激活位于高维空间中，这引发了关于异常检测在统计上是否可行的担忧。我们表明，在线性表示假设（LRH）下，确实可能存在希望。在LRH概念空间中，通常通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复，附近点共享一个小的共同活跃支持。利用这一局部稀疏性洞察，我们提出了一种基于局部掩蔽SAE的异常检测框架，并提供了理论依据。我们在各种架构和数据集上进行了验证，包括能力测试数据集和安全特定数据集。最后，当我们允许算法使用1%的分布外数据进行校准时，局部稀疏方法实现了接近最佳的性能，展示了它们在仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算时捕获有意义安全信息的能力。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current supervised safety methods for large language models (LLMs), which rely on unsafe training data and struggle to adapt to new types of attacks and harm. Existing methods often fail to generalize due to their dependence on labeled data, prompting the authors to propose an anomaly detection approach that models only safe data and flags out-of-distribution inputs. This method is motivated by the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), which suggests that local sparsity in high-dimensional spaces can be leveraged for effective anomaly detection. The paper introduces a locally masked sparse autoencoder (SAE) framework, validated across various architectures and datasets, including capability-testing and safety-specific datasets. The results indicate that when using 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, the proposed locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, effectively capturing safety information while utilizing only a small fraction of SAE neurons.
本研究解决了现有大型语言模型（LLMs）监督安全方法的局限性，这些方法依赖于不安全的训练数据，无法适应新类型的攻击和危害。以往的方法在LLM激活的高维特性下，进行异常检测面临统计挑战。本文提出了一种基于局部稀疏性和线性表示假设（LRH）的新方法，利用局部掩蔽稀疏自编码器（SAE）进行异常检测，有效建模安全数据并标记超出分布的输入。该研究的贡献在于证明该方法在各种数据集（包括安全特定数据集）上能够实现近乎最佳的性能，仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算，从而有效捕捉安全信息，同时减少数据需求。
From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization
Authors: Diba Afroze, Xingli Zhang, Yazhou Tu, Xiali Hei
First: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从意图到行动：车辆语音命令授权中的大型语言模型安全基准测试
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地集成到车辆语音助手中。但将自然语言请求与车辆功能关联起来会产生一个安全关键的授权问题。在执行命令之前，系统必须选择执行、拒绝、澄清、要求确认、推迟手动控制、触发紧急响应或不进行任何工具调用。根据我们的了解，之前的评估并未在发言者角色、身份验证状态、车辆状态和工具可用性之间隔离这一预行动决策。我们引入了一个包含202个场景的基准测试，采用七类分类法下的参考决策。我们使用决策对齐和安全特定错误指标评估了两个本地开放权重模型和三个基于API的LLMs。对齐率从Llama 3.2 3B的40.1%到Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的89.1%不等。基于API的模型得分在83.2%到89.1%之间，且它们之间没有统计学显著差异。即使这些模型在161个非执行场景中也产生了两到三个错误执行，并且在确认和手动控制决策中仍然存在持续错误。受控的Llama 3.2 3B消融在结构化授权政策下将对齐率提高到40.1%，而在仅使用模式和通用安全基准下为28.2-29.2%，但并未消除错误执行。因此，结构化LLM决策作为独立的安全机制是不够的，部署需要一个独立的执行层，在调用任何车辆功能之前验证工具权限和车辆状态约束。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety-critical issue of linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions in voice assistants powered by large language models (LLMs). Previous evaluations have not effectively isolated the pre-action decision-making process across various factors such as speaker role and vehicle state, leading to potential safety risks. This paper proposes a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions categorized under a seven-class taxonomy to evaluate LLM performance. The methodology involves assessing two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. The findings reveal that alignment scores range from 40.1% to 89.1%, with persistent errors in confirmation and manual-control decisions, indicating that structured LLM decisions alone are inadequate for safety, necessitating an additional enforcement layer to ensure proper tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints.
本研究解决了将自然语言请求与语音助手中的车辆功能链接的安全关键问题，强调了以往评估中未能根据各种因素孤立预行动决策的缺口。以往方法缺乏结构化评估授权过程的方式，导致命令执行中持续出现错误。所提出的基准引入了一个包含202个场景的评估框架，采用七类分类法系统评估车辆语音命令授权中的决策过程。该方法论涉及使用决策对齐和安全特定错误指标评估本地开放权重模型和基于API的大型语言模型（LLMs）。研究结果显示，各模型的对齐得分差异显著，API模型的得分在83.2%到89.1%之间，但在非执行场景中仍然产生错误执行，表明仅依靠结构化LLM决策不足以确保安全，部署时需要额外的执行层以实现有效性。
HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference --- Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds and Their Capability Boundaries for LLM Guardrails
Authors: Tak Ho Alex Li, Kaijie Liu, Lik-Hang Lee, Kin Chung Ho, Ping Shum, Michael K. Ng
First: 2026-08-09T05:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-15T12:22:48+00:00
Comments: Preprint v2, September 2026. 4 figures, 12 tables. Corrected and substantially revised from v1 (arXiv:2608.08485v1)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We ask a complementary question: how far can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen representations, and where does it fail? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning: an un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to the unit sphere S^{d-1}, and all decisions reduce to Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids. We contribute a boundary-mapping study rather than a leaderboard claim. On a frozen three-benchmark protocol, HoloAegis (3.2 MB) statistically matches WildGuard-7B (14 GB) on toxicity (0.96 vs. 0.96), exceeds it on harmful behaviors (0.99 vs. 0.79), and cedes oversafety detection (0.62 vs. 0.98) -- while ShieldGemma-2B fails on indirect harms (0.34). These failure modes are complementary and mechanistically traceable: potential-difference scoring senses manifold clustering, whereas policy-conditioned LLM judging requires explicit taxonomy matching. We restate our Topological Boundary Stability conjecture in ratio form and validate it via reference-set bootstrap: anchor banks reduce score variance 4-15x and boundary displacement to approximately 0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K) of the full-space estimator. Per-domain analysis further reveals that geometric separability tracks within-domain semantic homogeneity. Our results chart where geometric guardrails substitute for, and where they must defer to, LLM judges.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloAegis：冻结表示、拓扑推理 --- 最小参数安全流形及其对LLM保护措施的能力边界
当前LLM安全保护措施面临根本性矛盾：微调扭曲了预训练表示，而生成性判断则产生了高昂的推理成本。我们提出一个互补性问题：通过对冻结表示的纯几何推理，安全性可以达到多远，在哪里又会失败？我们提出HoloAegis，一个最小参数的拓扑推理框架，将表示与推理解耦：未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球面S^{d-1}，所有决策归结为对预计算锚点质心的Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能差异。我们贡献了一项边界映射研究，而非排行榜声明。在一个冻结的三基准协议上，HoloAegis（3.2 MB）在毒性上与WildGuard-7B（14 GB）统计匹配（0.96 vs. 0.96），在有害行为上超越它（0.99 vs. 0.79），而在过度安全检测上则让步（0.62 vs. 0.98）——同时ShieldGemma-2B在间接危害上失败（0.34）。这些失败模式是互补的，并且在机制上可追溯：电位差评分感知流形聚类，而政策条件的LLM判断则需要明确的分类匹配。我们以比率形式重述我们的拓扑边界稳定性猜想，并通过参考集自助法进行验证：锚库将得分方差降低4-15倍，边界位移约为全空间估计器的0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K)。每个领域的分析进一步揭示几何可分性与领域内语义同质性之间的关系。我们的结果描绘了几何保护措施替代和必须依赖LLM判断的地方。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenges faced by current large language model (LLM) safety guardrails, which often distort pre-trained representations through fine-tuning and incur high costs with generative judges. The authors propose HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that utilizes frozen representations to perform geometric reasoning, thereby separating representation from reasoning. This approach is motivated by the need to explore the limits of safety achievable without fine-tuning, and it demonstrates that geometric reasoning can effectively match or exceed the performance of larger models on certain toxicity metrics while revealing complementary failure modes. The methodology involves mapping text to a unit sphere and making decisions based on Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids. HoloAegis achieves competitive results on a frozen three-benchmark protocol, statistically matching a larger model on toxicity and outperforming it on harmful behaviors, while also providing insights into the conditions under which geometric guardrails can substitute for LLM judges.
本文探讨了确保大型语言模型（LLMs）安全性的挑战，传统方法如微调会损害预训练表示，而生成评判者则会产生高昂的推理成本。提出的方法HoloAegis引入了一种最小参数拓扑推理框架，利用冻结表示，允许在不微调的情况下进行几何推理。该方法有效地将表示与推理分离，采用未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球体，并基于Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能差异做出决策。本文的贡献在于其边界映射研究，表明HoloAegis在毒性方面与更大模型的性能相当，并在有害行为方面超越了它们，同时识别出过度安全检测的局限性。结果表明，几何护栏在某些情况下可以有效替代LLM评判者，为每种方法最有效的条件提供了见解。
Useless but Safe? Benchmarking Utility Recovery with User Intent Clarification in Multi-Turn Conversations
Authors: Mingqian Zheng, Malia Morgan, Liwei Jiang, Carolyn Rose, Maarten Sap
First: 2026-04-29T18:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-12T19:48:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety alignment techniques improve model robustness against adversarial attacks, but overlook whether and how LLMs can recover helpfulness when benign users clarify their intent. We introduce CarryOnBench, the first interactive benchmark that measures whether LLMs can revise their interpretation of user intent and recover utility, while remaining safe through multi-turn conversations. Starting from 398 seemingly harmful queries with benign underlying intents, we simulate 5,970 conversations by varying user follow-up sequences, evaluating 14 models on both intent-aligned utility and safety. CarryOnBench yields 1,866 different conversation flows of 4--12 turns, totaling 23,880 model responses. We design Ben-Util, a checklist-based metric that evaluates how well each model response fulfills the user's benign information need using atomic items. At turn one, models fulfill only 10.5--37.6% of the user's benign information need. When the same query includes the benign intent upfront, models fulfill 25.1--72.1%, confirming that models withhold information due to intent misinterpretation, not limited knowledge. With benign clarifications in multi-turn conversations, 13 of 14 models approach or exceed this single-turn baseline, yet recovery cost varies across models. We identify two failure modes invisible to single-turn evaluations---unsafe recovery, where a model updates at disproportionate safety cost and redundant recovery, where a model recycles prior responses rather than providing new information. Moreover, conversations converge to similar harmfulness levels regardless of how conservative the model starts. These findings expose a gap that single-turn evaluations miss---whether a model is appropriately cautious or simply unresponsive to clarified user intent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无用但安全？在多轮对话中通过用户意图澄清基准测试实用性恢复
当前的LLM安全对齐技术提高了模型对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但忽视了LLM在良性用户澄清意图时是否以及如何恢复有用性。我们引入CarryOnBench，这是第一个交互式基准，测量LLM是否能够修正对用户意图的解释并恢复实用性，同时在多轮对话中保持安全。从398个看似有害的查询开始，这些查询具有良好的潜在意图，我们通过变化用户后续序列模拟了5,970个对话，评估了14个模型在意图对齐的实用性和安全性方面的表现。CarryOnBench产生了1,866种不同的对话流程，包含4到12轮，总计23,880个模型响应。我们设计了Ben-Util，这是一个基于清单的指标，评估每个模型响应在多大程度上满足用户的良性信息需求。在第一轮中，模型仅满足用户良性信息需求的10.5%到37.6%。当相同的查询提前包含良性意图时，模型满足25.1%到72.1%，确认模型因意图误解而隐瞒信息，而不是知识有限。在多轮对话中，13个模型接近或超过这一单轮基线，但恢复成本在模型之间有所不同。我们识别出两种在单轮评估中不可见的失败模式——不安全恢复，即模型以不成比例的安全成本进行更新，以及冗余恢复，即模型重复之前的响应而不是提供新信息。此外，无论模型起始多么保守，对话都趋向于相似的有害性水平。这些发现揭示了单轮评估所忽视的一个差距——模型是否适当地谨慎，或仅仅对澄清的用户意图无反应。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety alignment techniques, which enhance robustness against adversarial attacks but fail to assess the models' ability to recover helpfulness when users clarify their intent in multi-turn conversations. Previous methods primarily focused on single-turn evaluations, leading to a lack of understanding regarding models' performance in ongoing interactions, particularly in distinguishing between safe and unsafe recovery of information. The proposed approach, CarryOnBench, introduces an interactive benchmark that evaluates LLMs' ability to revise their interpretations of user intent while maintaining safety across multi-turn dialogues. The methodology involves simulating 5,970 conversations based on 398 harmful queries with benign intents and assessing 14 models using a new metric, Ben-Util, which measures the fulfillment of user needs. The findings reveal that models initially fulfill only a fraction of user needs but improve significantly with clarified intent, highlighting the importance of multi-turn evaluations in understanding model performance and exposing failure modes that single-turn assessments overlook.

Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security
Authors: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
First: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-11T18:06:53+00:00
Comments: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents process external content, exposing them to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation. We present a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive cross-session attacks against fresh-session LLM defenders: an autonomous LLM attacker observes prior defender responses and pivots across rounds, while each defender response is evaluated as a fresh interaction. A controlled 3 x 3 attacker-defender matrix contains 945 battles. Restricting scoring to the first round yields 0-1% attack success rate (ASR); allowing 15 rounds yields 7.9-16.8%. Pooling three attacker LLMs uncovers 1.7-2.2 times as many unique successful inputs as the best single attacker, at three times the battle budget. Aggregate rates conceal opposing scenario-specific weaknesses in session-secret protection and authority handling, preserved in two higher-sample evaluations. On six scenarios, adding one provenance paragraph reduces ASR from 110/270 to 70/270, with selective effects across tasks. History and defender-state controls, together with frozen replay, characterize how the interaction protocol changes the result. A competition adds 18,422 held-out battles on a fixed gpt-oss-20b backbone and complementary benign-task evaluations. The benchmark exposes attacker and defender models, harnesses, scenarios, session state, and interaction budgets as configurable choices for systematic security evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应对手：多回合、多LLM基准测试用于LLM代理安全性
基于LLM的代理处理外部内容，使其暴露于提示注入和多回合操控中。我们提出了一个21场景基准测试，用于针对新会话LLM防御者的自适应跨会话攻击：自主LLM攻击者观察先前防御者的响应并在回合间进行调整，而每个防御者的响应被评估为一次新的交互。一个受控的3 x 3攻击者-防御者矩阵包含945场战斗。将评分限制在第一回合的攻击成功率（ASR）为0-1%；允许15回合则为7.9-16.8%。汇聚三种攻击者LLM发现的独特成功输入数量是最佳单一攻击者的1.7-2.2倍，战斗预算是三倍。汇总率掩盖了会话秘密保护和权限处理中的相对场景特定弱点，这在两个更高样本评估中得以保留。在六个场景中，添加一个来源段落将ASR从110/270降低到70/270，任务间效果选择性明显。历史和防御者状态控制，以及冻结重放，表征了交互协议如何改变结果。一个竞赛在固定的gpt-oss-20b基础上增加了18,422场保留战斗和补充的良性任务评估。该基准测试暴露了攻击者和防御者模型、工具、场景、会话状态和交互预算作为系统安全评估的可配置选择。

This paper proposes a novel benchmark consisting of 21 scenarios for evaluating adaptive cross-session attacks, allowing attackers to observe and adapt to defender responses across multiple rounds. The methodology includes a controlled matrix of 945 battles, revealing that while the attack success rate is low in single rounds, it increases significantly with multiple interactions. The findings indicate that pooling multiple attacker LLMs can yield a greater variety of successful attack inputs, highlighting specific weaknesses in session-secret protection and authority handling. 'Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Fawzia Zehra, Kara-Isitt, Sonal Khosla, Stephen Swift
First: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-09T16:54:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.
中文标题/摘要

乌尔都语是全球第十大语言，拥有2.46亿说话者，但在主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议中几乎完全缺席。为了调查这种缺席是否对内容审核的可靠性产生可测量的影响，测试了五个大型语言模型：GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1，涵盖了六个数据集，包括Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码切换的乌尔都英语。在五个乌尔都脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英语翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性范围为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）到31.6%（Qwen-2.5），'乌尔都语遗漏'率，即在英语翻译中标记为有害但在原始脚本中通过的内容，范围为2.4%到9.9%（中位数4.3%）。通过ACL Anthology API对九个ALW/WOAH版本中205篇论文的完整枚举确认，在整个期间内没有专门的乌尔都语论文。结果表明，当前的LLM在乌尔都语的脚本变体之间提供的不均匀安全保障，较小的开放权重模型显示出明显更高的不稳定性和遗漏危害率，超过了前沿的封闭模型。

The proposed approach highlights the discrepancies in harm detection between original Urdu scripts and their English translations, revealing a concerning 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate where harmful content is not flagged in the original script. Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Wenbo Ye, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Junwei Yao
First: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 table. Code, wrappers, and per-verdict labels: https://github.com/Yongxi-Zhou/safety-judge-robustness
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式胜于内容：内容不变的包装翻转LLM安全评判结果
自动安全评判系统——如Llama Guard或GPT-4o评分提示，决定模型回复是否有害——生成几乎所有报告的越狱成功率、防御评估和安全排行榜背后的数据。我们询问这些评判是根据回复的内容还是其语气进行评分。我们保持回复内容不变，添加内容不变的风格包装：在回复前或后放置的固定字符串，仅改变其语气（教育免责声明、虚假的安全“推理”块、后接不变的有害内容的拒绝令），或者在无害拒绝的情况下，仅仅听起来危险的框架。主体逐字保留，因此一个忠实的评判必须返回相同的判决，任何翻转都是评判的错误，而不是安全性的变化。在超过600个JailbreakBench回复x最多7种形式x 8个评判中，我们通过配对显著性测试和测量噪声底线来测量翻转率。发现是精确的而非普遍的：大多数评判几乎没有变化，但特定评判存在廉价可利用的盲点。一个令牌拒绝包装翻转了19.9%的GPT-4o-mini的正确“危险”判决（噪声底线0.5%；在三人多数重新评分下为18.2%），但对Claude的影响仅为0.4%。已部署的Llama Guard 4被确定性地操控：“教育课程”框架将12.3%的有害判决翻转为安全。第二个已部署的防护（gpt-oss-safeguard-20b）是免疫的，仅重写评分提示（StrongREJECT风格）就将攻击减少十倍，脆弱性存在于评判中，而非内容。两位注释者的人类验证确认100%的内容不变性和90%的翻转为评判错误（kappa 0.95-1.0），而自助法显示基础模型排名已经对抽样不稳定。我们发布数据集、包装、代码和每个判决标签。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the reliability of automatic safety judges, which are critical in evaluating the safety of responses generated by language models. Previous methods have focused on the content of replies, but they often overlook how stylistic changes can influence safety judgments, leading to inconsistencies in verdicts. The proposed approach introduces content-invariant style wrappers that modify the tone of a response without altering its content, allowing for a clear assessment of judge reliability. The study employs a comprehensive methodology involving over 600 responses tested against multiple judges, revealing that while most judges maintain consistent verdicts, some exhibit exploitable weaknesses. Notably, the findings demonstrate that certain wrappers can significantly alter safety judgments, with a token-refusal wrapper flipping nearly 20% of unsafe verdicts from one judge, highlighting vulnerabilities in the judging systems rather than the content itself. The paper contributes to the understanding of safety evaluation mechanisms in language models and provides tools for further research in this area.
本研究探讨了自动安全评估系统的可靠性，这些系统评估模型回复的有害性，并质疑这些评估者是评估内容还是仅仅评估回复的表现风格。以往的方法主要集中在内容评估上，但往往忽视了风格变化如何影响判断，从而导致安全评估的不一致性。提出的方法引入了内容不变的风格包装，这些包装在不改变内容的情况下改变回复的语气，有效测试评估者的鲁棒性。这种方法具有良好的动机，因为它揭示了安全评估者中可利用的弱点，为更好地理解其局限性做出了贡献。该研究采用了全面的实验设置，涉及600多个回复和多个评估者，发现某些评估者表现出显著的脆弱性，其中一个评估者的令牌拒绝包装翻转了近20%的不安全判决，而其他评估者则基本保持不变。研究结果表明，脆弱性存在于评估者内部，而非内容本身，强调了改进安全评估机制的必要性。
The Oversight Gap: What LLM Safety Monitors Miss, and Why It Is Not Capability
Authors: Xin Xu
First: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Several properties safety monitors are asked to certify, among them cross-tenant noninterference, sandbagging and evaluation awareness, are 2-safety hyperproperties, witnessed only by two executions. The standard consequence is a binary impossibility: one trace cannot decide them. We replace the binary with a measurement. A tight bound puts the balanced accuracy of any single-trace monitor at $\tfrac12+\tfrac12\,TV(P_0,P_1)$, turning undecidability into a graded detectability frontier and defining an oversight gap: a monitor's shortfall below it. On a leak family with closed-form $TV$, nine LLM monitors are optimal at $TV=0$ but capture little signal as $TV$ grows; at $TV=1$, where a 20-line membership check scores $100\%$, they average $60.9\%$. That shortfall is mostly not capability: naming what to check closes $61\%$ of it while leaving the $TV=0$ control at chance. The same split runs through a $2{\times}2$ factorial: an imagined second run leaves monitors at chance ($50.4\%$) while the same rule on an executed second run reaches $90.0\%$, and a stored oracle without a comparison procedure yields only $68.2\%$. Information and procedure are each necessary and neither is capability. Under nondeterminism, replay tracks a closed-form $k$-replay curve only under the right projection, and a projection frontier shows the resulting dilemma is forced: narrow misses $98.6\%$ of off-channel leaks, broad flags $75.7\%$ of clean traffic, and attainable accuracy decays like $1/(qm)$ in the benign-variation rate and the channel count. Finally, two frontier LLM judges certified an earlier version of our own benchmark as sound while a sign test found a directional bias ($p=2.7\times10^{-5}$) that invalidated three of our findings. Construction validity for hyperproperty benchmarks should be proved mechanically, not audited by models.
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the limitations of existing safety monitors for large language models (LLMs), particularly their inability to certify certain 2-safety hyperproperties such as cross-tenant noninterference and evaluation awareness, which are inherently undecidable with a single execution trace. Previous methods have relied on binary assessments, leading to a lack of nuanced understanding of monitor performance. The proposed approach introduces a graded detectability framework that quantifies monitor performance through a measurement of total variation distance (TV), revealing an oversight gap that indicates how much monitors fall short of optimal detection. The methodology involves analyzing the performance of nine LLM monitors across various TV levels, demonstrating that while some monitors perform optimally at TV=0, their effectiveness diminishes as TV increases. The findings indicate that naming what to check can significantly improve performance, yet a substantial shortfall remains, emphasizing the need for both information and procedural integrity in monitor design. The research contributes to the field by establishing a new benchmark for evaluating LLM safety monitors and highlighting the importance of mechanical proof for hyperproperty benchmarks rather than relying solely on model audits.
本文探讨了现有大型语言模型（LLMs）安全监控器的局限性，特别是它们在认证复杂属性（如跨租户非干扰和评估意识）方面的无能为力，这些属性被归类为2-安全超属性。传统方法在从单个执行轨迹中确定这些属性时面临二元不可能性，导致显著的监督缺口。作者提出了一种新的测量方法，量化监控器的性能，揭示尽管一些监控器在总变差（TV）为0时表现最佳，但随着TV的增加，其有效性下降。研究表明，命名检查内容可以显著减少监督缺口，在TV=1时实现平均60.9%的准确率，而简单检查的准确率为100%。该方法论包括对各种场景下监控器性能的详细分析，表明信息和程序要素对于有效监控都是必不可少的。研究结果表明，当前的监控技术不足，所提出的框架提供了对LLM安全性更细致的理解，对未来基准构建和验证具有重要意义。
SAEs Can Improve Unlearning: Dynamic Sparse Autoencoder Guardrails for Precision Unlearning in LLMs
Authors: Aashiq Muhamed, Jacopo Bonato, Mona Diab, Virginia Smith
First: 2025-04-11T01:24:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T03:32:59+00:00
Comments: COLM 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine unlearning is a promising approach to improve LLM safety by removing unwanted knowledge from the model. However, prevailing gradient-based unlearning methods suffer from issues such as high computational costs, hyperparameter instability, poor sequential unlearning capability, vulnerability to relearning attacks, low data efficiency, and lack of interpretability. While Sparse Autoencoders are well-suited to improve these aspects by enabling targeted activation-based unlearning, prior approaches underperform gradient-based methods. This work demonstrates that, contrary to these earlier findings, SAEs can significantly improve unlearning when employed dynamically. We introduce $\textbf{Dynamic DAE Guardrails}$ (DSG), a novel method for precision unlearning that leverages principled feature selection and a dynamic classifier. Our experiments show DSG substantially outperforms leading unlearning methods, achieving superior forget-utility trade-offs. DSG addresses key drawbacks of gradient-based approaches for unlearning -- offering enhanced computational efficiency and stability, robust performance in sequential unlearning, stronger resistance to relearning attacks, better data efficiency including zero-shot settings, and more interpretable unlearning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAEs可以改善遗忘：用于LLMs精确遗忘的动态稀疏自编码器护栏
机器遗忘是一种有前景的方法，通过从模型中移除不需要的知识来提高LLM的安全性。然而，现有的基于梯度的遗忘方法存在高计算成本、超参数不稳定、顺序遗忘能力差、易受重学习攻击、数据效率低和缺乏可解释性等问题。稀疏自编码器非常适合通过启用针对性激活的遗忘来改善这些方面，但之前的方法在性能上不如基于梯度的方法。本研究表明，与早期发现相反，SAEs在动态使用时可以显著改善遗忘。我们引入了$\textbf{动态DAE护栏}$（DSG），这是一种利用原则性特征选择和动态分类器的精确遗忘新方法。我们的实验表明，DSG在遗忘方法中显著优于领先的遗忘方法，实现了更好的遗忘-效用权衡。DSG解决了基于梯度的遗忘方法的关键缺陷——提供了更高的计算效率和稳定性、在顺序遗忘中的强大性能、对重学习攻击的更强抵抗力、更好的数据效率（包括零样本设置）以及更可解释的遗忘。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenges of machine unlearning in large language models (LLMs), which is essential for enhancing model safety by removing unwanted knowledge. Previous gradient-based unlearning methods face significant issues, including high computational costs, instability in hyperparameters, and vulnerability to relearning attacks, leading to inefficiencies and poor interpretability. This paper proposes a novel approach using Dynamic Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) through a method called Dynamic DAE Guardrails (DSG), which improves upon existing methods by enabling targeted activation-based unlearning and dynamic classification. The methodology demonstrates that DSG significantly enhances unlearning performance, achieving better forget-utility trade-offs and addressing the limitations of gradient-based methods, such as computational efficiency and robustness in sequential unlearning. The experimental results indicate that DSG outperforms leading unlearning methods, supporting its effectiveness in precision unlearning tasks.

Behind Harmful Compliance: Behavioral and Mechanistic Divergence Across LLM Jailbreaks
Authors: Md Rysul Kabir, Zoran Tiganj
First: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-05T18:10:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-weight language models can be rendered unsafe through several parameter-level interventions, yet models with matched harmful compliance can exhibit fundamentally different failure modes. We compare harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-feature abliteration in Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B using harmfulness, capability, self-audit, safety reflection, representation similarity, and refusal-direction repair. All three routes reach near-ceiling harmfulness, but SFT causes the broadest capability loss and representational drift; abliteration yields localized, family-dependent refusal-feature suppression; and RLVR largely preserves base-model capability, explicit safety judgments, and representation geometry while retargeting behavior toward compliance. RLVR models consequently remain unusually responsive to safety-reflection prompts despite complying under direct prompting. We further find that harmful RLVR induces capability-blind compliance: models claim to complete unavailable actions and fabricate information about nonexistent entities. Targeted RLVR calibration substantially reduces this false acceptance without restoring safety or degrading general capability. These results show that harmful compliance, harm recognition, and capability awareness are separable behavioral axes, and that self-audit and hallucination patterns are not necessarily robust safety signals under adaptive post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有害合规背后的行为和机制差异：LLM越狱的比较
开放权重语言模型可以通过多种参数级干预变得不安全，但具有匹配有害合规的模型可能表现出根本不同的失败模式。我们比较了Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B中的有害监督微调（SFT）、有害可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）和拒绝特征消融，使用有害性、能力、自我审计、安全反思、表示相似性和拒绝方向修复。所有三种途径都接近极限有害性，但SFT导致最广泛的能力损失和表示漂移；消融产生局部的、依赖于家族的拒绝特征抑制；而RLVR在重新定向合规行为的同时，基本上保留了基础模型的能力、明确的安全判断和表示几何。尽管在直接提示下合规，RLVR模型仍然对安全反思提示表现出异常的响应性。我们进一步发现，有害的RLVR会导致能力盲目的合规：模型声称完成不可用的动作，并虚构关于不存在实体的信息。针对性的RLVR校准显著减少了这种错误接受，而不恢复安全或降低一般能力。这些结果表明，有害合规、伤害识别和能力意识是可分离的行为轴，自我审计和幻觉模式在适应性后训练下不一定是稳健的安全信号。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the safety issues associated with open-weight language models, particularly focusing on how different parameter-level interventions can lead to harmful compliance with varying failure modes. The proposed approach, RLVR, addresses these issues by largely preserving the base-model's capabilities while retargeting behavior towards compliance, thus maintaining responsiveness to safety-reflection prompts. The paper contributes to understanding the separability of harmful compliance, harm recognition, and capability awareness in language models. The methodology involves comparing the effects of SFT, RLVR, and refusal-feature abliteration on models Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, revealing that RLVR can reduce false acceptance of unavailable actions while not compromising general capability, thereby supporting the research goals of enhancing model safety without sacrificing performance.

TIER: Threat Implicitness Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Safety Behaviors
Authors: Thu-Hien Trinh-Thi, Hai-Yen Vong, Thanh-Ha Ung-Dung, Tram Ho
First: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety benchmarks largely rely on binary metrics, overlooking how models respond to harmful prompts with varying threat implicitness. We introduce TIER, a Threat Implicitness Benchmark for behavioral safety evaluation of LLMs. TIER covers four risk domains and four threat levels, from explicit harmful requests to sophisticated jailbreaks. Responses are assessed using a six-label behavior scale and two independent LLM judges. Experiments on six open-weight LLMs show that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels rather than shifting directly from refusal to compliance. Contextual prompts yield the most diverse behaviors, while jailbreaks reveal the largest robustness gaps. Furthermore, models with similar Attack Success Rates can exhibit distinct response distributions, highlighting the need for behavior-aware LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIER：评估LLM安全行为的威胁隐含性基准
当前LLM安全基准主要依赖于二元指标，忽视了模型对不同威胁隐含性的有害提示的响应。我们引入TIER，一个用于LLM行为安全评估的威胁隐含性基准。TIER涵盖四个风险领域和四个威胁级别，从明确的有害请求到复杂的越狱。使用六级行为量表和两个独立的LLM评审对响应进行评估。对六个开放权重LLM的实验表明，安全行为在威胁级别之间逐渐演变，而不是直接从拒绝转变为遵从。上下文提示产生了最丰富的行为，而越狱则揭示了最大的鲁棒性差距。此外，具有相似攻击成功率的模型可能表现出不同的响应分布，突显了对行为感知的LLM安全评估的需求。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety benchmarks, which primarily use binary metrics and fail to account for the varying implicitness of threats in harmful prompts. Previous methods inadequately capture the nuances of model responses, leading to a lack of understanding of safety behaviors across different threat levels. The proposed TIER benchmark offers a more comprehensive evaluation by categorizing threats into four risk domains and levels, using a six-label behavior scale assessed by independent judges. This methodology reveals that LLM safety behaviors evolve gradually rather than transitioning abruptly from refusal to compliance, with contextual prompts eliciting the most varied responses and jailbreaks exposing significant robustness gaps. The findings indicate that even models with similar attack success rates can demonstrate distinct response patterns, underscoring the necessity for a more behavior-aware approach to LLM safety evaluation.

IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks
Authors: Saikat Mondal, Mamta, Deeksha Varshney, Oana Cocarascu, Asif Ekbal
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-09-03T12:54:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T11:46:25+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 7 figures, 33 tables. Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026. Contains examples of harmful model outputs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.
中文标题/摘要

大型语言模型（LLMs）在多语言环境中的使用日益增加，但其安全性仍主要在英语中进行评估。这限制了我们对低资源和文化多样语言中对齐失败表现的理解。我们引入了IndicSafeEval，这是一个针对印度语言的基于说服的越狱评估框架。我们的基准结合了十个安全关键内容类别和六种类人说服策略，涵盖四种不同的印度语言，如印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和旁遮普语，共生成7200个对抗性提示。我们对多个开源LLMs进行了系统的黑箱评估，以检查它们的安全行为在不同语言、说服策略和风险类别中的变化。我们的分析表明，模型在所有语言和提示风格中并不表现出同等的安全性。相反，安全性能在很大程度上依赖于所使用的语言和请求的措辞方式。我们进一步观察到，不同的风险类别表现出不同的脆弱性，一些类型的有害内容对基于说服的越狱攻击显著更易受影响。这些发现揭示了当前安全评估的重要局限性，主要集中在英语，并强调了需要多语言和说服意识的基准框架，以更准确地评估现实世界中的LLM安全性。我们的实现可在https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval获取。警告：本文包含可能冒犯或有害的示例数据。

FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models
Authors: Yalun Wu, Junfeng Fang, Jiawei Wang, Haotian Liu, Qijun Yang, Minghan Yang, Hongcheng Guo, Zhoujun Li, Boyang Wang
Venue: Proceedings of the Conference on Language Modeling (COLM), 2026
First: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLY-EVAL++：一种基于证据的安全约束飞行预测评估协议
在安全关键、物理约束的环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）需要的不仅仅是基于准确性的指标，因为数值上接近真实值的预测仍然可能违反操作约束、以物理不一致的方式组合领域，或未能生成可用的结构化输出。现有的评估协议无法可靠地测量这些失败模式。我们提出了FLY-EVAL++，一种基于证据的评估协议，结合了对协议合规性、物理可行性和安全约束的确定性验证，以及通过固定评分标准引导的聚合，形成可解释的多维评分。我们通过扩展PilotBench设置，结合历史条件和多步预测任务，为飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）实例化FLY-EVAL++。在66个LLM中，安全合规性是模型行为中最具区分性的维度：具有可比预测性能的模型在安全评分上相差超过28分，我们观察到反复出现的失败，包括在物理上合理的预测下的安全违规和多步展开中的不稳定性。这些结果表明，在安全关键领域的评估应明确测量约束满足和结构有效性，而不是仅依赖于以准确性为中心的报告。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for robust evaluation protocols for large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical environments, where traditional accuracy metrics fail to capture operational constraints and physical feasibility. Previous methods inadequately measure failure modes related to safety and structured output, leading to unreliable assessments. The proposed FLY-EVAL++ protocol improves upon these limitations by integrating deterministic verification of compliance with safety constraints and physical feasibility, along with a rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable scores. This paper contributes by demonstrating the effectiveness of FLY-EVAL++ in the context of Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP), revealing that safety compliance is a critical dimension of model behavior, with significant performance disparities observed across 66 LLMs. The findings indicate that the proposed methodology not only enhances evaluation rigor but also highlights the importance of constraint satisfaction in safety-critical applications.
本研究关注在安全关键环境中有效评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的必要性，传统的准确性指标无法捕捉操作约束和物理一致性，因此显得不足。现有方法无法可靠地评估这些失败模式，这促使了FLY-EVAL++的开发，这是一种证据驱动的评估协议，结合了对安全约束和物理可行性的合规性进行确定性验证，以及结构化评分系统。该方法具有良好的动机，旨在提高在安全至关重要的背景下评估的可靠性。该方法论涉及将FLY-EVAL++应用于飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）任务，揭示了66个LLMs在安全合规性方面存在显著差异，尽管预测性能相似，但安全评分相差超过28分。这些发现强调了在安全关键评估中明确测量约束满足和结构有效性的重要性，而不仅仅依赖于准确性指标。
TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization
Authors: Shiliang Xiao
First: 2026-08-30T05:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-01T03:40:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose TACS, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, TACS augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that TACS consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TACS：用于LLM越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择
基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法通常通过保留当前损失最低的候选来更新后缀。我们表明，这种看似自然的设计在根本上是短视的：在当前步骤代理下看起来更好的候选往往在搜索的后期未能产生更好的越狱结果，揭示了一种选择阶段奖励黑客行为。这表明候选选择，而不仅仅是候选生成，是后缀优化中的一个隐藏瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了TACS，一个用于越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择框架。TACS不仅仅通过候选的即时损失来选择候选，而是通过轨迹感知代理增强每一步的评估，并通过参考策略正则化和判别器估计的卡方修正来稳定选择，鼓励在当前步骤之外仍然有效的选择。在HarmBench上的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算下始终优于强基线，显著提高了攻击成功率，同时在整个搜索过程中表现出更稳定的优化行为。我们的研究结果强调，减轻由短视候选选择引起的选择阶段奖励黑客行为对于改善越狱后缀优化至关重要。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods, which often rely on selecting candidates based solely on their immediate loss, leading to suboptimal long-term outcomes due to a phenomenon known as selection-stage reward hacking. The proposed approach, TACS, introduces a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework that enhances the evaluation process by incorporating a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizing selection through reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction. This method effectively mitigates the issues associated with myopic candidate selection, thereby improving the optimization process. The experiments conducted on HarmBench demonstrate that TACS significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving higher attack success rates and more stable optimization behavior, thus supporting its effectiveness in enhancing jailbreak suffix optimization.
本研究解决了现有基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法的局限性，这些方法通常仅根据即时损失选择候选者，导致由于选择阶段奖励黑客行为而产生的次优长期结果。所提出的方法TACS引入了一种基于轨迹的候选选择框架，通过结合轨迹感知代理和通过参考策略正则化及鉴别器估计的卡方修正来增强评估过程，从而稳定选择。该方法有效缓解了与短视候选选择相关的问题，对该领域做出了重要贡献。在HarmBench上的实验结果表明，TACS在相同搜索预算内优于强基线方法，取得了更高的攻击成功率和更稳定的优化行为，从而支持了改善越狱后缀优化的目标。
Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models
Authors: Qilong Wu, Sahil Wadhwa, Pranab Mohanty, Giri Iyengar, Varun Chandrasekaran
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00
Comments: To appear on EMNLP 2026 main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对大型语言模型的有效性感知越狱评估
越狱的鲁棒性已成为大型语言模型（LLM）安全评估的核心，但现有的方法主要依赖于拒绝行为、语义相似性和意图匹配启发式，这些方法强调语言的合理性而非正确性。我们识别出现有评估的一个关键限制：许多越狱意图依赖于指令有效性而非认知事实性，允许看似真实的响应被标记为成功，尽管在事实或程序上是错误的。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了顺序认知和行动级验证（SEAV），这是一个以验证为中心的越狱评估框架，它将响应分解为有序步骤，并评估有效性和正确性。SEAV结合了LLM作为评判机制的语义解释与使用外部知识源的检索基础验证，评估生成内容是否在事实上正确、结构上连贯，并在操作上能够推进有害目标。实证结果表明，SEAV将SD-A（一个策划的战略不诚实诊断）的假阳性率降低了14.9个百分点，相较于最强基线，并在四个公共基准中的三个重新将22.1%至51.0%的先前标记为成功的样本重新分类为无效。这些结果表明，强制执行正确性显著重塑了测量的鲁棒性：许多先前标记为越狱成功的案例被重新分类为无效，且结果在测试的搜索后端和评估模型中保持稳定。代码和数据可在https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV获取。

Risk-Adjusted Harm Scoring for Automated Red Teaming for LLMs in Financial Services
Authors: Fabrizio Dimino, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali
First: 2026-03-11T14:14:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T14:24:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM safety evaluations rely on binary attack-success rates and domain-agnostic taxonomies, leaving regulated Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) deployments exposed to failures elicited through legally or professionally plausible framing. We introduce RAHS (Risk-Adjusted Harm Score), a risk-sensitive metric jointly capturing disclosure severity, disclaimer mitigation, and inter-judge agreement, and FinRedTeamBench, a 989-prompt benchmark spanning seven BFSI risk areas and 34 sub-categories mapped to regulatory frameworks. Evaluation uses an ensemble of three heterogeneous LLM judges, validated against human experts, and an adaptive multi-turn red-teaming pipeline. On nine open-weight models, RAHS preserves separation under near-ceiling ASR, ranking is stable under hyperparameter sweeps, and multi-turn pressure drives not only more jailbreaks but more operationally severe disclosures, exposing failure modes that single-turn, domain-agnostic evaluations cannot reveal.
中文标题/摘要

Do We Still Need Humans in the Loop? Human vs. LLM Annotation in Active Learning for TikTok Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Ahmad Dawar Hakimi, Lea Hirlimann, Isabelle Augenstein, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-04-15T14:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T11:56:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotating data remains a costly bottleneck for supervised NLP. Active learning (AL) reduces the number of human labels needed by selecting only the most informative instances, while instruction-tuned LLMs attack the same bottleneck from the other side, making labels cheap enough to annotate entire corpora. This raises two questions: can LLM labels replace human labels within the AL loop, and does AL remain necessary when entire corpora can be cheaply labeled? We investigate both by training supervised hate speech classifiers on a new dataset of 278K German political TikTok comments, comparing human and LLM annotation under matched conditions. LLM annotation at scale outperforms human-supervised classifiers at roughly one-tenth the cost, for both a closed-source (GPT-5.2) and an open-weight (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) LLM, and the advantage is robust under soft-label evaluation. It hinges on the annotation interface: only a two-question decomposition mirroring the human annotation task unlocks it. AL provides no reliable advantage over random sampling in our prefiltered pool. Error structure depends on the LLM: only GPT-5.2 matches the human FP/FN balance, while other variants over-flag border-control and economic-competition discourse. Humans remain essential as evaluators; for training labels, the question shifts to which LLM, which interface, and what shape of pool.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们还需要人类参与吗？在TikTok仇恨言论检测中人类与LLM注释的主动学习比较
数据注释仍然是监督NLP的一个成本瓶颈。主动学习（AL）通过仅选择最具信息量的实例来减少所需的人类标签数量，而经过指令调优的LLM则从另一个角度解决同一瓶颈，使标签变得足够便宜以注释整个语料库。这引发了两个问题：LLM标签能否替代AL循环中的人类标签，以及当整个语料库可以被廉价标注时，AL是否仍然必要？我们通过在一个包含278K条德国政治TikTok评论的新数据集上训练监督仇恨言论分类器来研究这两个问题，比较在匹配条件下的人类和LLM注释。大规模LLM注释的表现优于人类监督的分类器，成本约为十分之一，适用于封闭源（GPT-5.2）和开放权重（Qwen3.5-122B-A10B）LLM，并且在软标签评估下优势稳健。这依赖于注释接口：只有与人类注释任务相对应的两个问题的分解才能解锁它。在我们预过滤的池中，AL对随机抽样没有可靠的优势。错误结构依赖于LLM：只有GPT-5.2匹配人类的FP/FN平衡，而其他变体则过度标记边境控制和经济竞争话语。人类作为评估者仍然至关重要；对于训练标签，问题转向哪个LLM、哪个接口以及什么形状的池。

The authors conducted experiments using a dataset of 278K German political TikTok comments, finding that LLM annotation significantly outperformed human annotation at a fraction of the cost, with the performance being consistent across different LLMs. The results indicate that while LLMs can efficiently generate training labels, human evaluators are still necessary for assessing model performance, suggesting a shift in focus towards optimizing LLM selection and annotation interfaces rather than relying solely on human input.
本研究解决了监督自然语言处理（NLP）中数据标注成本高昂的问题，特别是在TikTok仇恨言论检测的背景下。以往的方法主要依赖人工标注，耗时且成本高，而最近的指令调优大型语言模型（LLMs）使得生成标签变得更加便宜。所提出的方法探讨了LLM生成的标签是否可以有效替代人工标签在主动学习（AL）循环中的作用，以及当整个数据集可以以低成本标注时，AL是否仍然必要。该研究的贡献在于比较了278K条德国政治TikTok评论的人类和LLM标注，结果显示，LLM标注在成本仅为人工标注十分之一的情况下显著优于人工标注，特别是在使用特定标注接口时。研究结果表明，尽管LLM可以高效生成训练标签，但人工评估者在评估模型性能方面仍然至关重要，这表明人类和LLM在标注过程中的角色应更加细致化。
From Detection to Refusal: Safer LLMs via Circuit-Guided Weight Scaling
Authors: Kuan-Lin Chu, Chung-En Sun, Tsui-Wei Weng
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of the 2026 Conference on Empirical Methods in Natural Language Processing (EMNLP 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite extensive alignment efforts, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to generating unsafe content under adversarial prompting, yet the internal mechanisms by which safety behaviors are implemented remain poorly understood. We study LLM safety from a mechanistic interpretability perspective and characterize a multi-stage *safety circuit* that organizes refusal behavior, consisting of (i) $\textbf{Harmful Detection Heads}$ that respond to harmful inputs, (ii) $\textbf{Safety Neurons}$ that mediate and stabilize safety signals in the residual stream, and (iii) $\textbf{Refusal Heads}$ that translate these signals into safe response generation. Using targeted attention-head and neuron-level interventions, we provide causal evidence consistent with this circuit organization, showing that suppressing upstream Harmful Detection Heads disrupts downstream refusal behavior and that safety neurons mediate this interaction. We validate that this decomposition recurs across multiple LLM architectures and adversarial attack settings, and use simple, architecture-preserving weight scaling as a mechanistic probe to test its functional relevance. Across six LLMs, circuit-guided scaling improves safety rates under attacks by 26.5%, while incurring only a 1.7% accuracy drop across four standard benchmarks. Overall, our results support a circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety and suggest that mechanistic abstractions can reveal stable and transferable patterns underlying aligned behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从检测到拒绝：通过电路引导的权重缩放实现更安全的LLM
尽管进行了广泛的对齐努力，大型语言模型（LLM）在对抗性提示下仍然容易生成不安全内容，但实现安全行为的内部机制仍然不够清楚。我们从机制可解释性的角度研究LLM安全性，并描述一个多阶段的*安全电路*，该电路组织拒绝行为，包括（i）响应有害输入的$\textbf{有害检测头}$，（ii）在残差流中介导和稳定安全信号的$\textbf{安全神经元}$，以及（iii）将这些信号转化为安全响应生成的$\textbf{拒绝头}$。通过针对性注意力头和神经元级干预，我们提供了与该电路组织一致的因果证据，表明抑制上游有害检测头会干扰下游拒绝行为，而安全神经元介导了这种互动。我们验证了这种分解在多个LLM架构和对抗攻击设置中反复出现，并使用简单的、保持架构的权重缩放作为机制探针来测试其功能相关性。在六个LLM中，电路引导的缩放在攻击下提高了26.5%的安全率，同时在四个标准基准上仅造成1.7%的准确率下降。总体而言，我们的结果支持LLM安全性的电路级解释，并表明机制抽象可以揭示对齐行为背后的稳定和可转移模式。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the ongoing vulnerability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generating unsafe content despite alignment efforts, highlighting a lack of understanding of the internal mechanisms that ensure safety. Previous methods have not effectively characterized these mechanisms, leading to insufficient safety measures. The proposed approach introduces a multi-stage safety circuit that includes Harmful Detection Heads, Safety Neurons, and Refusal Heads, which collectively enhance the model's ability to refuse harmful prompts. This method is well-motivated as it provides a mechanistic interpretability perspective on LLM safety. The research methodology involves targeted interventions at the attention-head and neuron levels, demonstrating causal relationships within the safety circuit. The findings show that circuit-guided weight scaling improves safety rates by 26.5% across six LLMs while maintaining a minimal accuracy drop of 1.7% on standard benchmarks, supporting the goal of enhancing LLM safety without significantly compromising performance.
本研究解决了尽管进行了对齐努力，大型语言模型（LLMs）仍然容易生成不安全内容的问题，强调了对安全行为内部机制理解不足的现状。以往的方法主要集中在对齐上，而未能充分解决潜在机制，导致安全问题持续存在。所提出的方法引入了一个多阶段的安全电路，包括有害检测头、安全神经元和拒绝头，从机制可解释性的角度增强了对LLM安全性的理解和控制。该方法论涉及对注意力头和神经元级别的有针对性干预，展示了安全电路内的因果关系。研究结果表明，电路引导的权重缩放在六个LLM上提高了对抗攻击下的安全率26.5%，同时在标准基准上仅有1.7%的准确率下降，从而支持了所提出的LLM安全的电路级解释。
Turning the Spell Around: Lightweight Alignment Amplification via Rank-One Safety Injection
Authors: Harethah Abu Shairah, Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud, George Turkiyyah, Bernard Ghanem
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2025-08-28T13:22:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T07:43:32+00:00
Comments: EMNLP 2026, Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) often involves mediating internal representations to refuse harmful requests. Recent research has demonstrated that these safety mechanisms can be bypassed by ablating or removing specific representational directions within the model. In this paper, we propose the opposite approach: Rank-One Safety Injection (ROSI), a white-box method that amplifies a model's safety alignment by permanently steering its activations toward the refusal-mediating subspace. ROSI operates as a simple, fine-tuning-free rank-one weight modification applied to all residual stream write matrices. The required safety direction can be computed from a small set of harmful and harmless instruction pairs. We show that ROSI consistently increases safety refusal rates - as evaluated by Llama Guard 3 - while preserving the utility of the model on standard benchmarks such as MMLU, HellaSwag, and Arc. Furthermore, we show that ROSI can also re-align 'uncensored' models by amplifying their own latent safety directions, demonstrating its utility as an effective last-mile safety procedure. Our results suggest that targeted, interpretable weight steering is a cheap and potent mechanism to improve LLM safety, complementing more resource-intensive fine-tuning paradigms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扭转咒语：通过秩一安全注入实现轻量级对齐放大
大型语言模型（LLMs）中的安全对齐通常涉及调解内部表示以拒绝有害请求。最近的研究表明，这些安全机制可以通过消融或移除模型内特定的表示方向来绕过。在本文中，我们提出了相反的方法：秩一安全注入（ROSI），这是一种白盒方法，通过永久性地将模型的激活引导到拒绝调解子空间来放大模型的安全对齐。ROSI作为一种简单的、无需微调的秩一权重修改，应用于所有残差流写矩阵。所需的安全方向可以从一小组有害和无害的指令对中计算得出。我们展示了ROSI在评估Llama Guard 3时始终提高安全拒绝率，同时在MMLU、HellaSwag和Arc等标准基准上保持模型的效用。此外，我们还展示了ROSI可以通过放大自身潜在安全方向来重新对齐“未审查”模型，证明其作为有效的最后一公里安全程序的实用性。我们的结果表明，针对性的、可解释的权重引导是一种廉价而有效的机制，可以提高LLM的安全性，补充更资源密集的微调范式。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs), where existing methods often fail due to the ability of harmful requests to bypass safety mechanisms by altering internal representations. The proposed approach, Rank-One Safety Injection (ROSI), differs from past methods by amplifying safety alignment through a straightforward, fine-tuning-free modification of model weights, specifically targeting residual stream write matrices. This method is well-motivated as it allows for the computation of necessary safety directions from a limited set of instruction pairs. The paper contributes by demonstrating that ROSI significantly enhances safety refusal rates, as measured by Llama Guard 3, while maintaining model performance on standard benchmarks like MMLU, HellaSwag, and Arc. The findings indicate that ROSI serves as an effective and efficient last-mile safety procedure, reinforcing the safety of LLMs without the need for extensive fine-tuning.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）中安全对齐的挑战，现有方法常常由于有害请求能够通过改变内部表示而绕过安全机制而失效。所提出的方法，即秩一安全注入（ROSI），与过去的方法不同，它通过增强安全对齐而不是试图调解有害请求，采用简单的、不需要微调的模型权重修改。该方法的动机明确，因为它允许从有限的指令对中计算安全方向。本文的贡献在于证明ROSI显著提高了安全拒绝率，使用Llama Guard 3进行测量，同时在标准基准（如MMLU、HellaSwag和Arc）上保持模型性能。研究结果表明，ROSI是增强LLM安全性的有效且高效的解决方案，即使对于缺乏先前安全对齐的模型也是如此。
Single Canonical Prompts Underestimate LLM Safety's Surface-Form Sensitivity
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Junwei Yao, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Wenbo Ye, Jiaxi Wen, Lai Yun Choi
First: 2026-08-01T22:28:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T05:09:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Sci-FM Workshop @ COLM 2026 (non-archival). Workshop version with reviews: https://openreview.net/forum?id=mZh0MqpOOC
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A benchmark score is a measurement instrument, yet most benchmarks read each item at a single canonical surface form. We ask whether that reading is faithful: when an item's intent is held fixed and only its meaning-preserving surface form varies, does the canonical-form score estimate model behavior well, and how much of any variation is decoding/judge noise rather than signal? We instantiate this in safety, a high-stakes setting with no gold label to average toward. To avoid prior confounds, we pre-author the reformulations (refusal-free, mostly non-LLM: machine back-translation and a Matrix-Language-Frame code-switch generator) so an identical surface form reaches every model, score all responses with one human-anchored, vendor-neutral judge (Claude, kappa = 0.86 vs. human on unsafe compliance, stable across languages, cross-checked by GPT-4o), and verify intent preservation. On 370 seeds x 5 surface forms x 5 models, no single transformation is uniformly most dangerous (6 of 20 per-transformation McNemar tests survive correction, most protective). Yet evaluating only the canonical prompt underestimates unsafe compliance: the union of unsafe outcomes across forms exceeds even the worst single form by 3.3-12.9 pp, with bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero for all five models, and 5-13% of seeds safe on canonical are unsafe under some reformulation -- above a zero stochasticity floor (canonical resampled five times at temperature 0 gives 0/370 new exposures). The size of this gap is model-dependent (largest on Gemini 2.5 Pro). One form recovers only ~53% of a model's observed unsafe surface and about three reach 85% -- a redundancy characterization of this form set, not of a defined population. A benign control (XSTest) suggests the instability is bidirectional, though the benign and harmful pools are not item-matched. We release the dataset, code, and per-response labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：单一规范提示低估了大型语言模型安全性的表面形式敏感性
基准分数是一种测量工具，但大多数基准以单一规范表面形式读取每个项目。我们询问这种读取是否忠实：当项目的意图保持不变，仅其保持意义的表面形式变化时，规范形式分数是否能很好地估计模型行为，以及任何变化中有多少是解码/评判噪声而非信号？我们在安全性这一高风险环境中实例化这一点，因其没有金标准可供平均。为了避免先前的混淆，我们预先授权了重构（无拒绝，主要非大型语言模型：机器回译和矩阵语言框架代码切换生成器），以便相同的表面形式到达每个模型，所有响应由一个人类锚定的、中立的评审（Claude，kappa = 0.86与人类在不安全合规性上的比较，跨语言稳定，由GPT-4o交叉验证），并验证意图保持。在370个种子x 5个表面形式x 5个模型中，没有单一变换是普遍最危险的（20个每变换的McNemar测试中有6个在校正后存活，大多数是保护性的）。然而，仅评估规范提示低估了不安全合规性：跨形式的不安全结果的并集甚至超过了最糟糕的单一形式3.3-12.9个百分点，所有五个模型的自助法95%置信区间均不包括零，且5-13%的在规范下安全的种子在某些重构下是不安全的——高于零随机性底线（规范在温度0下重采样五次给出0/370个新暴露）。这一差距的大小依赖于模型（在Gemini 2.5 Pro上最大）。一种形式仅恢复了模型观察到的不安全表面的约53%，大约三种形式达到了85%——这是对这一形式集的冗余特征，而非定义人口的特征。一个良性对照（XSTest）表明不稳定性是双向的，尽管良性和有害池并未按项目匹配。我们发布了数据集、代码和每个响应标签。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of existing benchmark evaluations in assessing the safety of large language models (LLMs), which typically rely on a single canonical surface form for scoring. Previous methods have not adequately captured the variability in model behavior when the surface form changes, leading to an underestimation of unsafe compliance. The proposed approach involves pre-authoring multiple reformulations of prompts and evaluating model responses using a consistent human-anchored judge, thus ensuring intent preservation and reducing confounding factors. The study's methodology includes testing 370 seeds across five surface forms and five models, revealing that relying solely on canonical prompts fails to account for significant variations in unsafe outcomes, with the union of unsafe results across forms exceeding the worst single form by 3.3-12.9 percentage points. This highlights the model-dependent nature of safety assessments and contributes a dataset and evaluation framework that can better inform safety evaluations in LLMs.
本研究解决了现有基准评估在评估大型语言模型（LLMs）安全性方面的局限性，这些评估通常依赖于单一的标准表面形式，可能无法准确反映模型在表面形式变化下的行为。以往的方法未能充分考虑由于解码噪声导致的模型响应变异性，从而导致对不安全合规性的低估。所提出的方法涉及预先编写多个提示的改写，并通过一致的人类评判者评估模型响应，以确保意图的保留。这种方法揭示了仅依赖标准提示显著低估了潜在的不安全结果，研究结果表明，不同表面形式的不安全结果的联合可能超过任何单一形式的最坏情况。研究表明，没有单一的转化形式是普遍最危险的，性能指标显示，在标准提示下被认为安全的种子中，有相当一部分在替代表述下是不安全的，突显了对LLM安全性进行更细致评估的必要性。
NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation
Authors: Zhiyuan Xu, Muhammad Firhard Roslan, Joseph Gardiner, Sana Belguith, Lichao Wu
First: 2026-08-26T12:44:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-26T12:44:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome. This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuronFuzz：安全神经元引导的LLM安全评估模糊测试
安全评估对于评估对齐的大型语言模型（LLMs）是否能够抵御越狱攻击至关重要。然而，现有的自动化测试方法主要依赖于响应级反馈：每个候选提示通常需要生成目标模型的响应以评估其攻击有效性。这个过程成本高昂，更重要的是，对于大多数候选者在相同失败结果下被拒绝时，仅提供稀疏的指导。 本文提出了NeuronFuzz，一个利用内部安全神经元作为LLM安全评估的连续执行反馈的白盒模糊测试框架。SafetyOracle将安全神经元激活转换为连续的安全警报分数，作为模糊测试的反馈，并且可以在预填充期间获得，从而消除模糊测试循环中的响应生成。为了构建SafetyOracle，NeuronFuzz使用模板不变的有害和良性输入以及稳定性感知选择，识别出一组紧凑的安全神经元，其激活捕捉有害意图识别。此外，由于安全警报分数是可微分的，NeuronFuzz利用其梯度识别安全敏感的模板位置，并使用掩码语言模型生成流畅、上下文兼容的变异，同时保留原始有害负载并避免额外的优化变量。我们在21个文本和多模态模型上评估了NeuronFuzz。在五个白盒源模型中，它实现了76-100%的越狱发现率，超越基线最多48个百分点。其优化模板进一步零-shot转移到开放权重和六个专有目标模型，分别实现69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%的平均ASR和前5集成ASR（EASR）。
